# 导数：考点 49-55 全量本地题库

这一页保留本地一轮复习资料的原题、公式图片与图形。它是课后抽题资料池，不替代前面的讲义和手写推导：第一次学习请先完成定义、定理和方法来源，再到这里限时训练。

返回专题首页：[导数](./README.md)。优先训练：[导数：分层训练与费曼讲题](./08_derivative_drills_zh.md)。

## 使用纪律

1. 先写定义域，再写导函数与符号表；不能跳过参数分类或端点检查。
2. 遇到恒成立、存在、唯一、恰有几个零点，先把量词翻译成范围、最值或函数图像语言。
3. 解析版原文只在点击后显示；其中的图片是原 Word 公式或图形，用于保留题目条件。

## 考点 49：利用导数求切线方程

原卷抽取 30 道，解析版抽取 30 道。

### 题 18（原题 2）

:::diagram

</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">2．已知点<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-170-1637ea1570.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="640">在函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-171-a5fbeae74c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1600" height="576">的图象上，则过点<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-172-376ad671b1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="416">的曲线<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-173-83026f13c1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2016" height="640">的切线方程是                                         。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-174-cf0b0068b3.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2176" height="512">或<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-175-744b44c027.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2272" height="512"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】由于点A(1,2)在函数f(x)=ax3的图象上，则a=2,即y=2x3，y′=6x2，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设切点为(m,2m3),则切线的斜率为k=6m2，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由点斜式得：y-2m3=6m2(x- m).</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">代入点A（l，2）得,2-2m3=6m2(1-m).</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">即有<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-176-f7bc104ef4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2752" height="512">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-177-63bf2f6b95.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3168" height="640">.解得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-178-8f82b338a7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">或<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-179-38c737c677.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="640" height="992">，即斜率为6或<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-180-4b852cd3c8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="992"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则过点A的曲线C:y=f(x)的切线方程是：</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">y−2=6(x−1)或y−2=<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-181-4b852cd3c8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="992">(x−1)，即6x−y−4=0或3x−2y+1=0.</p>
</div>
:::

:::

<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-184-a006c72ca5.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1632" height="512">或<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-185-e5475e11c9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2528" height="512"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】设切点为（m，m3-3m），<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-186-511728c61d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2304" height="576">的导数为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-187-a009ac0455.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2400" height="576">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">可得切线斜率k＝3m2-3，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由点斜式方程可得切线方程为y﹣m3+3m＝(3m2-3)（x﹣m），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">代入点<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-188-8892b3ad52.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1376" height="512">可得﹣6﹣m3+3m＝(3m2-3)（2﹣m），</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">当m=0时，切线方程为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-189-a006c72ca5.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1632" height="512">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当m=3时，切线方程为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-190-e5475e11c9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2528" height="512">。</p>
</div>
:::

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-question">

</div>

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">3．若函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-282-d45798c475.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="640">与<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-283-c1f1620e3b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2592" height="640">的图象存在公共切线，则实数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-284-3bea866181.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="320" height="352">的最大值为______</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】e</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】设公切线与f（x）＝x2+1的图象切于点（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-285-c405af7006.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="576">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-286-0ca5c99d04.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="992" height="608">），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">与曲线C：g（x）＝<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-287-b1b65af512.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">切于点（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-288-c386b1b980.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="416" height="576">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-289-391efc4786.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1600" height="576">），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴2<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-290-e317958d28.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="7200" height="1280">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">化简可得，2<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-291-cc132c0a68.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3040" height="1152">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-292-3cd05efb28.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2912" height="576"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∵2<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-293-ca915a8e12.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1280" height="1088">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">a<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-294-f88a620550.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2560" height="608">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设h（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-295-563bd4d02d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="512">（x＞0），则h′（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-296-b8316a9cbf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2304" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴h（x）在（0，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-297-5341d24db9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="576" height="576">）上递增，在（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-298-5341d24db9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="576" height="576">，+∞）上递减，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴h（x）max＝h（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-299-5341d24db9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="576" height="576">）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-300-587be19cb7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="576" height="352">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴实数a的的最大值为e，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">故答案为e．</p>
</div>
:::

### 题 27（原题 4）

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-question">
<p class="local-docx-line">4．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-q-073-160da84938.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="576">，若过点P（1，t）存在3条直线与曲线<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-q-074-65b55bcfeb.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="512">相切，求t的取值范围__________。</p>
</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">4．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-301-160da84938.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="576">，若过点P（1，t）存在3条直线与曲线<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-302-65b55bcfeb.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="512">相切，求t的取值范围__________。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】(-3，-1)</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】设过点P（1，t）的直线与曲线y＝f（x）相切于点（x，2x3﹣3x），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-303-7009a60e44.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2208" height="1056">6x2﹣3，化简得，4x3﹣6x2+3+t＝0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">令g（x）＝4x3﹣6x2+3+t，则令g′（x）＝12x（x﹣1）＝0，则x＝0，x＝1．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴g（x）在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-304-ea871ef901.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1280" height="448">（1，+<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv49-a-305-8ac1540e93.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="416" height="320">）上单增，在（0，1）上单减，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">且g（0）＝3+t，g（1）＝t+1，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">又∵过点P（1，t）存在3条直线与曲线y＝f（x）相切，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则（t+3）（t+1）＜0，解得，﹣3＜t＜﹣1．故答案为(-3，-1).</p>
</div>
:::

:::

:::diagram

<p class="local-docx-line">2．若函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-277-f62c9c0516.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3648" height="576">恰好有三个单调区间，则实数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-278-3bea866181.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="320" height="352">的取值范围是         。</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】∵函数f（x）＝ax3﹣3x2+x+1，∴f′（x）＝3ax2﹣6x+1，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由函数f（x）恰好有三个单调区间，得f′（x）有两个不相等的零点，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴3ax2﹣6x+1＝0满足：a≠0，且△＝36﹣12a＞0，解得a＜3，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴a∈（﹣∞，0）∪（0，3）．</p>
</div>
:::

:::

### 题 48（原题 4）

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-question">

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">3．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-280-c631ff14aa.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3584" height="576">,则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-281-b8bdd33b59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="768" height="480">在(1，3)上不单调的一个充分不必要条件是    。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">①<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-282-dd40f85e59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1824" height="992">②<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-283-bda97858ab.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="992">③<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-284-7bf876a735.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1728" height="928">④<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-285-44b5b2a53c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1632" height="992"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】③</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】f′（x）=2ax﹣4a﹣<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-286-38a2ebb782.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="992">=<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-287-720a6764b4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2272" height="1056">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">若f（x）在（1，3）上不单调，令g（x）=2ax2﹣4ax﹣1，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则函数g（x）=2ax2﹣4ax﹣l与x轴在（1，3）有交点，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">a=0时，显然不成立，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">a≠0时，只需<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-288-b22369ea5e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2816" height="1280">，解得：a＞<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-289-350998ee3b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="992">.</p>
</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">4．已知函数f(x)＝－<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-290-2abaf0ee58.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="352" height="992">x3＋bx2－(2b＋3)x＋2－b在R上不是单调递减函数，则b的取值范围是         。</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】f′(x)＝－x2＋2bx－(2b＋3)，要使原函数在R上单调递减，应有f′(x)≤0恒成立，∴Δ＝4b2－4(2b＋3)＝4(b2－2b－3)≤0，∴－1≤b≤3，故使该函数在R上不是单调递减函数的b的取值范围是b&lt;－1或b&gt;3.</p>

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">5．函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-430-b8bdd33b59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="768" height="480">在定义域<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-431-003dad7bad.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="384" height="416">内可导，若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-432-c9b0532992.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="512">，且当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-433-6e8c20d36b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1696" height="512">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-434-b1ba2b3329.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2432" height="576">，设<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-435-b2a0f1e5fd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="512">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-436-a9f528cd8e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="992">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-437-79ceff4c9e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="512">，则                  。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-438-8fc9a0a14c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】x∈（-∞，1）时，x-1＜0，由（x-1）•f&#x27;（x）＜0，知f&#x27;（x）＞0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以（-∞，1）上f（x）是增函数．∵f（x）=f（2-x），∴f（3）=f（2-3）=f（-1）</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以f（-1）＜（0）＜<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-439-76c617efc4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="992">，因此c＜a＜b．</p>
</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">8．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-501-0ecca2ce5e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">满足<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-502-7f52d95610.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2336" height="640">，且当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-503-705be6d221.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1760" height="640">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-504-7cb80538ff.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2848" height="640">成立，若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-505-fd10e2d589.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2848" height="704">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-506-04a08cf47a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2784" height="640">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-507-cf08f4747d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3552" height="1216">，则a，b，c的大小关系是       。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-508-19c1bb3136.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】根据题意，令h（x）＝xf（x），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">h（﹣x）＝（﹣x）f（﹣x）＝﹣xf（x）＝﹣h（x），则h（x）为奇函数；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当x∈（﹣∞，0）时，h′（x）＝f（x）+xf&#x27;（x）＜0，则h（x）在（﹣∞，0）上为减函数，又由函数h（x）为奇函数，则h（x）在（0，+∞）上为减函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以h（x）在R上为减函数，a＝（20.6）•f（20.6）＝h（20.6），b＝（ln2）•f（ln2）＝h（ln2），c＝（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-509-c1baea0189.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1056" height="992">）•f（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-510-c1baea0189.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1056" height="992">）＝h（<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-511-c1baea0189.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1056" height="992">）＝h（﹣3），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">因为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-512-819f0017ba.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="992">0＜ln2＜1＜20.6，则有<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv50-a-513-19c1bb3136.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">；</p>
</div>
:::

:::

<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-178-abb15bbe09.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2293" height="529"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】构造函数，令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-179-e2cab7bef4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3936" height="640">，则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-180-bcbeff3c54.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3968" height="1056">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-181-3eae320f21.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3296" height="992">可得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-182-3901729558.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1568" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-183-e2eaa7dff7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="640">是区间<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-184-fa8b63462a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1248" height="640">上的单调递减函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">且<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-185-1ef07ffa2c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3264" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当x∈(0,1)时,g(x)&gt;0,∵lnx&lt;0,f(x)&lt;0,(x2-1)f(x)&gt;0;</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当x∈(1,+∞)时,g(x)&lt;0,∵lnx&gt;0,∴f(x)&lt;0,(x2-1)f(x)&lt;0</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∵f(x)是奇函数,当x∈(-1,0)时,f(x)&gt;0,(x2-1)f(x)&lt;0</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴当x∈(-∞,-1)时,f(x)&gt;0,(x2-1)f(x)&gt;0.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">综上所述,使得(x2-1)f(x)&gt;0成立的x的取值范围是<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-186-a55a692e94.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2560" height="640">.</p>
</div>
:::

<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】设g（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-265-1cbcac9e6e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="992">，则g′（x）＝<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-266-3c11643f50.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="1056">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∵当x＞0时，xf′（x）﹣f（x）&gt;0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴当x＞0时，g′（x）&gt;0，此时函数g（x）为增函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∵f（x）是奇函数，∴g（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-267-1cbcac9e6e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="992">是偶函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">即当x＜0时，g（x）为减函数．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∵f（﹣1）＝0，∴g（﹣1）＝g（1）＝0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当x＞0时，f（x）&gt;0等价为g（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-268-1cbcac9e6e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="992">&gt;0，即g（x）&gt;g（1），此时x＞1，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当x＜0时，f（x）&gt;0等价为g（x）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-269-1cbcac9e6e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="992">&lt;0，即g（x）&lt;g（﹣1），此时﹣1＜x＜0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">综上不等式的解集为（﹣1，0）∪（1，+∞），</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【题组四  带常数不等号型】</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】令g(x)=f(x)+lnx (x&gt;0) ，则g&#x27;(x)= <img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-298-cd128e50f3.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="960"> ，</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">∵<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-303-eb4c438ae6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1664" height="640">，∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-304-05d60ce4c2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3456" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-305-bf77444c0b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="640">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-306-cd73df59d3.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="640">，当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-307-aee7c72aa9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1760" height="640">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv52-a-308-d5061128e5.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="640">，</p>

### 题 120（原题 1）

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-question">
<p class="local-docx-line">1．已知f（x）=2x3﹣6x2+m（m为常数），在[﹣2，2]上有最大值3，那么此函数在[﹣2，2]上的最小值为     ．</p>
</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">1．已知f（x）=2x3﹣6x2+m（m为常数），在[﹣2，2]上有最大值3，那么此函数在[﹣2，2]上的最小值为     ．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】﹣37</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】由已知，f′（x）=6x2﹣12x，有6x2﹣12x≥0得x≥2或x≤0，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">因此当x∈[2，+∞），（﹣∞，0]时f（x）为增函数，在x∈[0，2]时f（x）为减函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">又因为x∈[﹣2，2]，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以得当x∈[﹣2，0]时f（x）为增函数，在x∈[0，2]时f（x）为减函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以f（x）max=f（0）=m=3，故有f（x）=2x3﹣6x2+3</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以f（﹣2）=﹣37，f（2）=﹣5</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">因为f（﹣2）=﹣37＜f（2）=﹣5，所以函数f（x）的最小值为f（﹣2）=﹣37．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">答案为：﹣37</p>
</div>
:::

:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">1．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-268-8cfbd030ad.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="4288" height="992"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-269-2c7d4a3ce7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1152" height="512">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（1）讨论函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-270-b8bdd33b59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="768" height="480">的极值点的个数；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-271-b8bdd33b59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="768" height="480">有两个极值点<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-272-d65558a016.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="512" height="576"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-273-35de016fc8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="416" height="576">，证明：<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-274-f066f56e96.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2880" height="640">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】（1）答案不唯一，具体见解析；（2）证明见解析.</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">③当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-487-c34f6be868.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="832" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-488-9fd8f8206f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2240" height="448">，此时<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-489-d1e6c4d30c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1504" height="512">恒成立，</p>

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">5．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-517-9816d32247.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2816" height="576">，其中<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-518-c71229ba7c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="992" height="448">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-519-af0b688947.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="256" height="320">为自然对数的底数.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（1）当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-520-3868aa783d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="736" height="416">时，证明：对<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-521-526f7c58a6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2848" height="480">；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）若函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-522-b8bdd33b59.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="768" height="480">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-523-a3c2eca785.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1120" height="1088">上存在极值，求实数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-524-3bea866181.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="320" height="352">的取值范围。</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】(1)见证明；(2) <img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-525-1e31d45ed4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="640"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】(1)当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-526-8e9de46222.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="832" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-527-94bb9d7b20.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2592" height="640">，于是，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv53-a-528-51f82e164e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2752" height="640">.</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">由<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-310-a51142dc6d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="992">,解得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-311-af32fb9f34.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="960" height="992">，可知<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-312-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-313-72aa4f7c47.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1056" height="1088">上是增函数，在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-314-6b5f1272a0.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="1088">上是减函数.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-315-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">的极大值为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-316-b8483100cb.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2624" height="992">，无极小值.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-317-6e85d529db.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="12416" height="1056"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">①当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-318-4a390f7c80.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-319-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-320-72aa4f7c47.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1056" height="1088">和<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-321-c05610cf9d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="1088">上是增函数，在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-322-ca8cadc8f6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="1088">上是减函数；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">②当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-323-be3307681f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-324-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-325-9bc468bc24.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1248" height="640">上是增函数；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">③当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-326-0b11e2b923.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-327-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-328-e734476597.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1088" height="1088">和<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-329-6b5f1272a0.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="1088">上是增函数，在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-330-0edcf57405.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="1088">上是减函数</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（3）当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-331-44277c0b46.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">时，由（2）可知<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-332-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-333-4c3a7260fa.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="800" height="640">上是增函数，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-334-e39ab25b9c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="7680" height="992">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-335-00276bf372.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="5408" height="640">对任意的a∈（2, 3），x­1, x2∈[1, 3]恒成立，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-336-e7be3a0c38.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="5792" height="640"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-337-f92f20b8e5.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="6112" height="992">对任意<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-338-44277c0b46.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">恒成立，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-339-37a72bd4c2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1760" height="992">对任意<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-340-44277c0b46.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">恒成立，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">由于当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-341-44277c0b46.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-342-4e1d6ff99e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2688" height="992">，∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-343-e459959b6e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="992">.</p>
</div>

### 题 137（原题 5）

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-question">
<p class="local-docx-line">5．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-q-045-135b706336.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3776" height="576">．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（1）当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-q-046-e1313a7450.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="352">时，求曲线y=f（x）在点（1，f（1））处的切线与两坐标轴围成的三角形的面积；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）若f（x）≥1，求a的取值范围．</p>
</div>
:::

:::solution 查看解析版原文

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">5．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-344-135b706336.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3776" height="576">．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（1）当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-345-e1313a7450.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="352">时，求曲线y=f（x）在点（1，f（1））处的切线与两坐标轴围成的三角形的面积；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）若f（x）≥1，求a的取值范围．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】（1）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-346-3931b59996.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="800" height="992">（2）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-347-1640303ad9.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1408" height="640"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】（1）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-348-1e38e1b3cd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3264" height="576">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-349-935c38cd47.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2560" height="992">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-350-1dcad7b4e3.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2752" height="512">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-351-431de84bdd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="512">，∴切点坐标为(1,1+e),</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴函数f(x)在点(1,f(1)处的切线方程为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-352-dae9cf1c2b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3648" height="512">,即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-353-b4760ca8f0.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2432" height="640">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-354-68534231fa.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="352" height="320">切线与坐标轴交点坐标分别为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-355-0a9f2f42a4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2368" height="992">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴所求三角形面积为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-356-3e805ec2b3.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2976" height="992">;</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）解法一：<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-357-4999540f67.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="4128" height="576">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-358-04454d6cc1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2976" height="992">，且<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-359-cc924a260b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-360-cce42a64b6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2016" height="512">,则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-361-041512dfc6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3456" height="992"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴g(x)在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-362-320d843daf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="512">上单调递增，即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-363-9135ad76fd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="832" height="480">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-364-320d843daf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1184" height="512">上单调递增，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-365-4a248d6d89.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="736" height="416">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-366-bf02298e39.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="512">,∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-367-c3ae150f4c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2944" height="640">,∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-368-039fee0ef8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="640">成立.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-369-7181c400d6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="736" height="416">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-370-d8b69504b7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="992"> ，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-371-e1dc4da1df.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1536" height="768">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-372-5ca5dd10d1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="5440" height="1056">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴存在唯一<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-373-2434f1820a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1024" height="576">，使得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-374-2990fffec6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3616" height="1088">，且当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-375-8b88ecc741.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1600" height="576">时<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-376-b6f94180ae.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1504" height="512">，当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-377-78c2d3d6d1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2116" height="635">时<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-378-58a113eb8c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1504" height="512">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-379-7ce7ad723a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2048" height="1088">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-380-6252b58c48.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3424" height="576">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">因此<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-381-7858f1faef.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="5728" height="608"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-382-688938d6f1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="8928" height="1216">&gt;1,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-383-510542918d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1536" height="640">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-384-039fee0ef8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="640">恒成立；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-385-26358f2e4c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1376" height="448">时， <img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-386-bf92bddb46.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3424" height="512">∴<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-387-cba026c404.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2656" height="512">不是恒成立.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">综上所述，实数a的取值范围是[1,+∞).</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">解法二：<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-388-5ea4997382.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="7200" height="640">等价于</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-389-33f1fdfacf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="6016" height="512">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-390-d6358e1eef.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="640">,上述不等式等价于<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-391-1faadd58f0.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3616" height="640">,</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">显然<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-392-f70cb02964.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="640">为单调增函数，∴又等价于<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-393-56dd701529.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2464" height="448">，即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-394-51679a67b6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2464" height="448">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-395-c9b0a1bd69.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2688" height="640">,则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-396-f33de37cd2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3136" height="992"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-397-2526ddc33b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="640">上h’(x)&gt;0,h(x)单调递增；在(1,+∞)上h’(x)&lt;0,h(x)单调递减，</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">(2)由<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-417-6161087534.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2464" height="992">得，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-418-662a278a22.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3296" height="992">，其中<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv54-a-419-5cb6d35297.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">，</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-016-949d90716a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2304" height="640">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-017-77c7289af1.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1568" height="640">；当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-018-cee593541d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2304" height="640">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-019-887cee02cd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1568" height="640">，所以<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-020-324a59922b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-021-eb7a3adef7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1760" height="640">单调递减，在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-022-ba23bd83a2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1760" height="640">单调递增.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）（ⅰ）若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-023-f3d0d66806.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">，由（1）知，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-024-324a59922b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">至多有一个零点.</p>

:::diagram
<div class="local-docx-card local-docx-answer">
<p class="local-docx-line">1．已知函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-179-c6a571aab5.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2496" height="640">．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（1）若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-180-4a248d6d89.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="736" height="416">，证明：当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-181-5cb6d35297.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-182-8e23be5e4b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1622" height="705">；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）若<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-183-d8a9bca7f2.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-184-917b55115c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="51" height="27">只有一个零点，求<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-185-1bf2e8df7f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="320" height="352">的值.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】（1）见解析；（2）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-186-c9c79b9c47.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1088" height="1056"></p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】（1）当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-187-4a248d6d89.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="736" height="416">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-188-8e23be5e4b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1622" height="705">等价于<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-189-cbbe66d2b0.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2784" height="704">．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设函数<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-190-71ccbf128d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3392" height="704">，则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-191-b92152d410.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="6272" height="736">．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-192-0e4b9ea55a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="832" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-193-0d2f9b3baf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1568" height="640">，所以<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-194-c13a7e9356.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-195-9bc468bc24.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1248" height="640">单调递减．</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">而<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-196-68657735ca.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="640">，故当<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-197-5cb6d35297.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">时，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-198-f5933a4153.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1472" height="640">，即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-199-8e23be5e4b.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1622" height="705">．</p>

<p class="local-docx-line">【答案】（1）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-247-8d19c970fd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="1088">；（2）<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-248-134fb75c3d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2016" height="1088">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">【解析】（1）令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-249-836ece6f3d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2848" height="1056">，则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-250-782f270546.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1120" height="1056">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-251-a90bc9c1d7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1664" height="1056">，则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-252-30ac89b50d.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2464" height="1056">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-253-d39d47e74e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1504" height="640">，得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-254-c7cc2a0639.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1440" height="448">；</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">令<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-255-b1449b40b6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1504" height="640">，得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-256-e116665d5e.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="896" height="448">或<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-257-5a587883cf.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="800" height="416">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-258-b3690c331a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="640">在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-259-7786d3811f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="640">和<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-260-ac6709c7af.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1248" height="640">上单调递减，在<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-261-a4c083ace7.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="928" height="640">上单调递增，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">故<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-262-c9f58a9afe.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3264" height="672">，<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-263-e94f8c8d49.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3456" height="992">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">结合<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-264-b3690c331a.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="640">的图象</p>
<div class="local-docx-figure"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-block-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-265-a7be6c4d20.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="336" height="232"></div>
<p class="local-docx-line">可知<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-266-1bf2e8df7f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="320" height="352">的取值范围为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-267-8d19c970fd.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1216" height="1088">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">（2）不等式<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-268-27ed508094.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3392" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">即<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-269-564cedb8b4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3520" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">整理得<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-270-799aaa1ee8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2976" height="704">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">设<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-271-c1e92c9960.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="640">，则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-272-e80b137fd4.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2144" height="640">.</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">因为<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-273-aaa15665e6.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="864" height="448">，所以<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-274-b6f0d8c8cc.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2720" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">所以<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-275-eadcdb2161.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="3808" height="640">，</p>
<p class="local-docx-line">则<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-276-5d3d74486f.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="1824" height="1056">.设<img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-277-b024fc23e8.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="2464" height="1056">，则</p>
<p class="local-docx-line"><img class="local-docx-image local-docx-inline-image" src="../../../assets/derivative-local/dv55-a-278-95713fd08c.png" alt="本地解析几何资料图片" width="8352" height="1440">.</p>
